This feature implements that first stage in `internal/dupes`.\n\n## Interface\n\n`dupes.GroupBySize` takes the eligible files produced by `walk.Files` (relative path and size) and\nreturns size buckets. Only buckets with two or more files are returned. The size is taken from the\nwalk result; file contents are never opened in this stage. A file whose size is unique appears in\nno bucket and is therefore never opened for hashing.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Reads: the in-memory walk result only.\n- Writes: nothing.\n\n## Required Project Tests\n\nThe project test suite in `internal/dupes` contains `TestGroupBySizeOmitsUniqueSizes` (a unique\nsize produces no bucket) and `TestGroupBySizeKeepsSharedSizes` (two or more files with one size\nproduce one bucket holding all of them).\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC size-unique-and-shared ===\nIntent: Files with a unique size are never reported and a shared size with identical contents is reported.\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ndef write(root, rel, data):\n    p = os.path.join(root, rel)\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(p, \"wb\") as f:\n        f.write(data)\n\nshared = b\"dddddd\"\nunique = b\"cc\"\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:\n    write(root, \"d1.bin\", shared)\n    write(root, \"sub/d2.bin\", shared)\n    write(root, \"c.bin\", unique)\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(root)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(out.splitlines()))[1:]\n    paths = sorted(r[3] for r in rows)\n    assert paths == [\"d1.bin\", \"sub/d2.bin\"]\n=== END AC size-unique-and-shared ===\n\n=== AC tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:\n    write(root, \"a.bin\", b\"aaaa\")\n    write(root, \"b.bin\", b\"bbbb\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(root)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Hash same-size files with SHA-256 and form duplicate groups, skipping unreadable files with a stderr warning. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-SizeGrouping.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | dupes.GroupByDigest |\n| Consumes    | dupes.GroupBySize |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nRule 3 groups same-size files by the SHA-256 digest of their contents; a group of two or more files\nwith the same size and digest is a duplicate group. Rule 6 skips unreadable files with one warning.\n\n## Interface\n\n`dupes.GroupByDigest` takes the size buckets from `dupes.GroupBySize`. For each bucket it opens each\nfile read-only and streams it through `crypto/sha256`. The digest is the lowercase hexadecimal\nencoding of the full contents. Files with identical size and digest form one group; groups with a\nsingle member are dropped.\n\nA file that cannot be opened or read yields exactly one warning line on standard error, is excluded\nfrom grouping, and does not change the exit code. Files are opened read-only; nothing is written,\nmoved, or deleted under the examined directory.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Reads: the contents of files in shared-size buckets.\n- Writes: warning lines to standard error only.\n\n## Required Project Tests\n\n`internal/dupes` contains `TestGroupByDigest` (identical contents group, same-size different\ncontents do not) and `TestGroupByDigestSkipsUnreadable` (an unreadable file is skipped with one\nwarning and does not fail the call).\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC digest-columns-match-contents ===\nIntent: Reported size and sha256 are the byte length and lowercase SHA-256 of the fixture contents.\n\nimport csv\nimport hashlib\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ndef write(root, rel, data):\n    p = os.path.join(root, rel)\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(p, \"wb\") as f:\n        f.write(data)\n\ncontent = b\"quarterly report\\n\"\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:\n    write(root, \"a/one.txt\", content)\n    write(root, \"b/two.txt\", content)\n    write(root, \"c/three.txt\", content)\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(root)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(out.splitlines()))[1:]\n    assert len(rows) == 3\n    for r in rows:\n        assert int(r[1]) == len(content)\n        assert r[2] == hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()\n    assert sorted(r[3] for r in rows) == [\"a/one.txt\", \"b/two.txt\", \"c/three.txt\"]\n=== END AC `./`.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Reads: the in-memory groups.\n- Writes: nothing.\n\n## Required Project Tests\n\n`internal/dupes` contains `TestOrder` covering reclaimable-first ordering, digest group.\n\n## Interface\n\n`report.WriteListing` writes to standard output through `encoding/csv` with `\\n` line endings\n(`UseCRLF` false). The header row is `group,size,sha256,path`. One row follows per file of every\nordered group: group number, size in bytes, lowercase hexadecimal SHA-256, relative path. With no\nduplicates only the header is written. Fields containing a comma, a double quote, or a newline are\nquoted as RFC 4180 specifies, by the csv writer.\n\nThe default (non-summary) mode of `cli.Run` runs the walk, size grouping, digest grouping, and\nordering with the configured `--min-size`, then this writer, and returns 0. `--min-size` excludes\nfiles smaller than the given number of bytes.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Reads: size and keep only sizes shared by two or more files, so unique-size files are never read. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-Walk.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | dupes.GroupBySize |\n| Consumes    | walk.Files |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nRule 3 of the build instructions says files are grouped by size first and only files that share a\nsize are read. This feature implements that first stage in `internal/dupes`.\n\n## Interface\n\n`dupes.GroupBySize` takes the eligible files produced by `walk.Files` (relative path and size) and\nreturns size buckets. Only buckets with two or more files are returned. The size is taken from the\nwalk result; file contents are never opened in this stage. A file whose size is unique appears in\nno bucket and is therefore never opened for hashing.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Reads: the in-memory walk result only.\n- Writes: nothing.\n\n## Required Project Tests\n\nThe project test suite in `internal/dupes` contains `TestGroupBySizeOmitsUniqueSizes` (a unique\nsize produces no bucket) and `TestGroupBySizeKeepsSharedSizes` (two or more files with one size\nproduce one bucket holding all of them).\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC size-unique-and-shared ===\nIntent: Files with a unique size are never reported and a shared size with the SHA-256 digest of their contents; a group of two or more files\nwith the same size and digest is a duplicate group. Rule 6 skips unreadable files with one warning.\n\n## Interface\n\n`dupes.GroupByDigest` takes the size buckets from `dupes.GroupBySize`. For each bucket it opens each\nfile read-only and streams it through `crypto/sha256`. The digest is the lowercase hexadecimal\nencoding of the full contents. Files with identical size and digest form one group; groups with a\nsingle member are dropped.\n\nA file that cannot be opened or read yields exactly one warning line on standard error, is excluded\nfrom grouping, and does not change the exit code. Files are opened read-only; nothing is written,\nmoved, or deleted under the examined directory.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Reads: the contents of files in shared-size buckets.\n- Writes: warning FEATURE: Group Ordering\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Compute reclaimable bytes per group, order and number the groups, and order paths within each group. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-DigestGrouping.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | dupes.Order |\n| Consumes    | dupes.GroupByDigest |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nRules 4 and 5 fix how paths are written and how groups and paths are ordered so output is\ndeterministic.\n\n## Interface\n\n`dupes.Order` takes the duplicate groups and returns them ordered and numbered.\n\n- Reclaimable bytes of a group are `size x (files - 1)`.\n- Groups are ordered by reclaimable bytes descending, then by digest ascending, and numbered from 1\n  in that order.\n- Within a group, paths are ordered by byte value ascending (plain string comparison).\n- Paths are relative to the examined directory, use `/` as the separator, and carry no leading `./`.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Reads: the in-memory groups.\n- Writes: nothing.\n\n## Required Project Tests\n\n`internal/dupes` contains `TestOrder` covering reclaimable-first ordering, digest tie-breaking,\nand byte-order paths.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC order-by-reclaimable-then-digest ===\nIntent: Groups appear by reclaimable bytes descending then digest ascending and are numbered from 1.\n\nimport csv\nimport hashlib\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ndef write(root, rel, data):\n    p = os.path.join(root, rel)\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(p, \"wb\") as f:\n        f.write(data)\n\nfixtures = {\n    b\"0123456789\": [\"big/a\", \"big/b\"],\n    b\"xyz\": [\"s/1\", \"s/2\", \"s/3\", \"s/4\", \"s/5\"],\n    b\"tie-aa\": [\"t1/a\", \"t1/b\"],\n    b\"tie-bb\": [\"t2/a\", \"t2/b\"],\n    b\"tie-cc\": [\"t3/a\", \"t3/b\"],\n}\ngroups = []\nfor content, paths in fixtures.items():\n    groups.append((len(content) * (len(paths) - 1), hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(), len(content), sorted(paths)))\nexpected = sorted(groups, key=lambda g: (-g[0], ARTIFACT FEATURE-Listing.md ===\n# FEATURE: Listing\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Write the CSV duplicate listing with header group,size,sha256,path using encoding/csv and wire the default mode of cli.Run. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-GroupOrdering.md, FEATURE-ModuleBuild.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | report.WriteListing |\n| Consumes    | dupes.Order |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe default output of `dupfind <directory>` is a CSV listing of every file in every duplicate group.\n\n## Interface\n\n`report.WriteListing` writes to standard output through `encoding/csv` with `\\n` line endings\n(`UseCRLF` false). The header row is `group,size,sha256,path`. One row follows per file of every\nordered group: group number, size in bytes, lowercase hexadecimal SHA-256, relative path. With no\nduplicates only the header is written. Fields containing a comma, a double quote, or a newline are\nquoted as RFC 4180 specifies, by the csv writer.\n\nThe default (non-summary) mode of `cli.Run` runs the walk, size grouping, digest grouping, and\nordering with the configured `--min-size`, then this writer, and returns 0. `--min-size` excludes\nfiles smaller than the given number of bytes.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Reads: ordered groups.\n- Writes: CSV to standard output only.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC listing-full-output ===\nIntent: The entire standard output equals the CSV the standard csv writer produces from the fixture-derived rows.\n\nimport csv\nimport hashlib\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ndef write(root, rel, data):\n    p = os.path.join(root, rel)\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(p, \"wb\") as f:\n        f.write(data)\n\nfixtures = {\n    b\"alpha alpha\\n\": [\"a/one.txt\", \"b/two.txt\", \"c/deep/three.txt\"],\n    b\"beta\\n\": [\".hidden/.env\", \"env.bak\"],\n}\nheader = [\"group\", \"size\", \"sha256\", \"path\"]\ngroups = []\nfor content, paths in fixtures.items():\n    groups.append((len(content) * (len(paths) - 1), hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(), len(content), sorted(paths)))\ngroups.sort(key=lambda g: (-g[0], g[1]))\nbuf = io.StringIO()\nw = csv.writer(buf, lineterminator=\"\\n\")\nw.writerow(header)\nfor n, g in enumerate(groups, start=1):\n    for p in g[3]:\n        w.writerow([n, g[2], g[1], p])\nexpected =   | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Write the one-row CSV summary of groups, duplicate files, and reclaimable bytes when --summary is given. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-Listing.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | report.WriteSummary |\n| Consumes    | dupes.Order |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nWith `--summary` the tool reports the totals of the listing that the same options would produce,\ninstead of the listing itself.\n\n## Interface\n\n`report.WriteSummary` writes, through `encoding/csv` with `\\n` line endings, the header\n`groups,duplicate_files,reclaimable_bytes` and one row:\n\n- `groups`: the number of duplicate groups.\n- `duplicate_files`: the sum of `files - 1` over all groups.\n- `reclaimable_bytes`: the sum of every group's `size x (files - 1)`.\n\nWith no duplicates the row is `0,0,0`. The `--summary` mode of `cli.Run` uses the same walk,\ngrouping, and ordering as the listing, with the same `--min-size`, and returns 0.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Reads: ordered groups.\n- Writes: CSV to standard output only.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC summary-totals-match-fixture ===\nIntent: The summary row equals totals computed from the fixture's known duplicate structure.\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ndef write(root, rel, data):\n    p = os.path.join(root, rel)\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(p, \"wb\") as f:\n        f.write(data)\n\nfixtures = {\n    b\"quarterly report\\n\": [\"a/r.txt\", \"b/r2.txt\", \"c/r3.txt\"],\n    b\"la la la\\n\": [\"m/s.mp3\", \"m/s2.mp3\"],\n    b\"unique content here\\n\": [\"u/solo\"],\n}\ndup_groups = [(c, p) for c, p in fixtures.items() if len(p) > 1]\nwant_groups = len(dup_groups)\nwant_files = sum(len(p) - 1 for c, p in dup_groups)\nwant_bytes = sum(len(c) * (len(p) - 1) for c, p in dup_groups)\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:\n    for content, paths in fixtures.items():\n        for p in paths:\n            write(root, p, content)\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--summary\", root)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(out.splitlines()))\n    assert len(rows) == 2\n    assert rows[0] == [\"groups\", \"duplicate_files\", \"reclaimable_bytes\"]\n    assert [int(x) for x in rows[1]] == [want_groups, want_files, want_bytes]\n=== END AC summary-totals-match-fixture ===\n\n=== AC summary-no-duplicates ===\nIntent: With no duplicates the summary row holds three zeros and exit status is 0.\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom
